theorem flipEdgePresent_boundary_side_package {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} {e f₃ f₄ : Finset V}
    (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
    (hU : UnitOn X σ)
    (hXc : bdry X = 0)
    (hMX : bdry M = X)
    (hT : IsTaut M)
    (hS : SimplicialChain M)
    (he : EligibleTet M e)
    (hexp : exposedFaces M e = {f₃, f₄})
    (hf₃₄ : f₃ ≠ f₄)
    (hFlip : FlipEdgePresent σ f₃ f₄) :
    ∃ A B : Finset V,
      A ∩ B = f₃ ∩ f₄ ∧
      (f₃ ∩ f₄).card = 2 ∧
      IsSphere2 ((flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ A)) ∧
      IsSphere2 ((flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ B)) ∧
      (∀ f ∈ flipBoundary σ M e, f ⊆ A ∨ f ⊆ B) ∧
      f₃ ⊆ A ∧ ¬ f₃ ⊆ B ∧ f₄ ⊆ B ∧ ¬ f₄ ⊆ A := by
  classical
  have hcd : (f₃ ∩ f₄).card = 2 := flipEdgePresent_cd_card he hexp hf₃₄
  have hmiss := flipEdgePresent_exposed_faces_missing hU hMX he hexp hf₃₄
  have hedges := flipEdgePresent_exposed_edges_in_sigma hU hMX he hexp hf₃₄ hFlip
  have hf₃card : f₃.card = 3 := by
    have hf₃mem : f₃ ∈ exposedFaces M e := by
      rw [hexp]
      simp only [Finset.mem_insert, Finset.mem_singleton, true_or]
    have hf₃tet : f₃ ∈ tetFaces e := exposedFaces_subset_tetFaces M e hf₃mem
    exact (Finset.mem_powersetCard.mp hf₃tet).2
  have hf₄card : f₄.card = 3 := by
    have hf₄mem : f₄ ∈ exposedFaces M e := by
      rw [hexp]
      simp only [Finset.mem_insert, Finset.mem_singleton, or_true]
    have hf₄tet : f₄ ∈ tetFaces e := exposedFaces_subset_tetFaces M e hf₄mem
    exact (Finset.mem_powersetCard.mp hf₄tet).2
  obtain ⟨A, B, W₃, W₄, hW₃, hW₄, hAB, hcover, hAeq, hBeq⟩ :=
    flipEdgePresent_compatible_triangle_cuts hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS he hexp hf₃₄ hFlip
  have hf₃flip : f₃ ∈ flipBoundary σ M e := by
    simp only [flipBoundary, Finset.mem_union]; right; rw [hexp]
    exact Finset.mem_insert_self f₃ _
  have hf₄flip : f₄ ∈ flipBoundary σ M e := by
    simp only [flipBoundary, Finset.mem_union]; right; rw [hexp]
    exact Finset.mem_insert_of_mem (Finset.mem_singleton_self f₄)
  refine ⟨A, B, hAB, hcd, ?_, ?_, hcover, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [hAeq]
    exact isSphere2_cut hσ hf₃card hedges.1 hmiss.1 hW₃
  · rw [hBeq]
    exact isSphere2_cut hσ hf₄card hedges.2 hmiss.2 hW₄
  · -- f₃ ⊆ A : the A-side filter is `insert f₃ (cutSet σ W₃)`, so `f₃` is in it
    have hmem : f₃ ∈ (flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ A) := by
      rw [hAeq]; exact Finset.mem_insert_self f₃ _
    exact (Finset.mem_filter.mp hmem).2
  · -- ¬ f₃ ⊆ B : else f₃ ∈ insert f₄ (cutSet σ W₄), but f₃ ≠ f₄ and f₃ ∉ σ ⊇ cutSet
    intro hf₃B
    have hmem : f₃ ∈ (flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ B) :=
      Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨hf₃flip, hf₃B⟩
    rw [hBeq, Finset.mem_insert] at hmem
    rcases hmem with h | h
    · exact hf₃₄ h
    · exact hmiss.1 (cutSet_subset h)
  · -- f₄ ⊆ B
    have hmem : f₄ ∈ (flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ B) := by
      rw [hBeq]; exact Finset.mem_insert_self f₄ _
    exact (Finset.mem_filter.mp hmem).2
  · -- ¬ f₄ ⊆ A
    intro hf₄A
    have hmem : f₄ ∈ (flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ A) :=
      Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨hf₄flip, hf₄A⟩
    rw [hAeq, Finset.mem_insert] at hmem
    rcases hmem with h | h
    · exact hf₃₄ h.symm
    · exact hmiss.2 (cutSet_subset h)


/-- **Case-2 edge split, geometry.** The already-present flip edge
`f₃ ∩ f₄` determines two vertex sides `A,B`, intersecting exactly in that edge,
such that the two filters of `flipBoundary σ M e` are 2-spheres, and every
remaining tetrahedron lies wholly on exactly one side.  This is the edge-split
analogue of `isSphere2_cut`. -/
theorem flipEdgePresent_side_sets {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} {e f₃ f₄ : Finset V}
    (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
    (hU : UnitOn X σ)
    (hXc : bdry X = 0)
    (hMX : bdry M = X)
    (hT : IsTaut M)
    (hS : SimplicialChain M)
    (he : EligibleTet M e)
    (hexp : exposedFaces M e = {f₃, f₄})
    (hf₃₄ : f₃ ≠ f₄)
    (hFlip : FlipEdgePresent σ f₃ f₄) :
    ∃ A B : Finset V,
      A ∩ B = f₃ ∩ f₄ ∧
      (f₃ ∩ f₄).card = 2 ∧
      IsSphere2 ((flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ A)) ∧
      IsSphere2 ((flipBoundary σ M e).filter (fun f => f ⊆ B)) ∧
      (∀ t ∈ (removeTet M e).support, t ⊆ A ∨ t ⊆ B) ∧
      (∀ t ∈ (removeTet M e).support,
        (t ⊆ A ∧ ¬ t ⊆ B) ∨ (t ⊆ B ∧ ¬ t ⊆ A)) ∧
      f₃ ⊆ A ∧ ¬ f₃ ⊆ B ∧ f₄ ⊆ B ∧ ¬ f₄ ⊆ A := by
  rcases flipEdgePresent_boundary_side_package hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS he hexp hf₃₄ hFlip with
    ⟨A, B, hAB, hcd, hsphereA, hsphereB, hfaceCover, hf₃A, hf₃notB, hf₄B, hf₄notA⟩
  rcases flipEdgePresent_removeTet_support_partition hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS he hexp hf₃₄ hFlip hAB hcd hfaceCover with ⟨hcover, hsep⟩
  exact ⟨A, B, hAB, hcd, hsphereA, hsphereB, hcover, hsep, hf₃A, hf₃notB, hf₄B, hf₄notA⟩

theorem bdry_filter_eq_filter_bdry_of_side_sep {V : Type*} [LinearOrder V] {M : Chain V} {A B : Finset V}
    (hpure : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t.card = 4)
    (hsep : ∀ t ∈ M.support,
      (t ⊆ A ∧ ¬ t ⊆ B) ∨ (t ⊆ B ∧ ¬ t ⊆ A))
    (hAB2 : (A ∩ B).card = 2) :
    bdry (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) = (bdry M).filter (fun f => f ⊆ A) := by
  classical
  ext f
  rw [Finsupp.filter_apply]
  by_cases hfA : f ⊆ A
  · rw [if_pos hfA]
    refine bdry_filter_apply_eq_bdry_of_no_cross (X := M) (P := fun t => t ⊆ A) (u := f) ?_
    intro t ht htA
    by_contra hbg
    obtain ⟨w, hw, hfw⟩ := exists_facet_of_bdryGen_ne_zero hbg
    have htB : t ⊆ B := by
      rcases hsep t ht with hside | hside
      · exact False.elim (htA hside.1)
      · exact hside.1
    have hfB : f ⊆ B := by
      rw [hfw]
      exact (Finset.erase_subset w t).trans htB
    have hfInter : f ⊆ A ∩ B := Finset.subset_inter hfA hfB
    have hcardf : f.card = 3 := by
      rw [hfw, Finset.card_erase_of_mem hw, hpure t ht]
    have hle : f.card ≤ (A ∩ B).card := Finset.card_le_card hfInter
    rw [hAB2] at hle
    omega
  · rw [if_neg hfA]
    rw [bdry_apply_eq_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun t ht => ?_
    rw [Finsupp.support_filter, Finset.mem_filter] at ht
    by_cases hgen : bdryGen t f = 0
    · rw [hgen, mul_zero]
    · exfalso
      obtain ⟨w, hw, hfw⟩ := exists_facet_of_bdryGen_ne_zero hgen
      have hfsubA : f ⊆ A := by
        rw [hfw]

    68	/-- Starting from a ball with boundary `B₀`, gluing on the tets of `l` in

   164	/-- **Ambient transport of a whole relative shelling.** Gluing the tets of `l` onto
   165	`B₀` transports to gluing them onto `B₀ ∪ K`, provided every tet's faces avoid `K`. -/
   166	lemma ShellFrom_union_disjoint {K : Finset (Finset V)} {l : List (Finset V)} :
   167	    ∀ {B₀ B : Finset (Finset V)}, ShellFrom B₀ l B →
   168	      (∀ t ∈ l, Disjoint (tetFaces t) K) → ShellFrom (B₀ ∪ K) l (B ∪ K) := by
   169	  induction l with
   170	  | nil =>
   171	      intro B₀ B h _
   172	      simp only [ShellFrom] at h ⊢
   173	      rw [h]
   174	  | cons t l ih =>
   175	      intro B₀ B h hd
   176	      simp only [ShellFrom] at h ⊢
   177	      obtain ⟨B₁, hg, hrest⟩ := h
   178	      exact ⟨B₁ ∪ K, hg.union_disjoint (hd t (List.mem_cons.mpr (Or.inl rfl))),
   179	        ih hrest (fun u hu => hd u (List.mem_cons.mpr (Or.inr hu)))⟩

   269	def RelShelling (τ B₀ B : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
   270	  ∃ l : List (Finset V), l.toFinset = τ ∧ l.Nodup ∧ ShellFrom B₀ l B

   272	/-- **Case-2 bridge (tautological given the relative shelling).** A ball `τ₁`,
   273	a `GlueStep` tet `t` onto its boundary, and a relative shelling of `τ₂` onto the
   274	resulting boundary, assemble into one ball. The hard part — that the separated
   275	ball actually supplies the `RelShelling` — is M22b. -/
   276	theorem IsBall.bridge_of_relShelling {τ₁ τ₂ B₁ Bmid B : Finset (Finset V)} {t : Finset V}
   277	    (h₁ : IsBall τ₁ B₁) (hg : GlueStep t B₁ Bmid) (h₂ : RelShelling τ₂ Bmid B)
   278	    (ht₁ : t ∉ τ₁) (ht₂ : t ∉ τ₂) (hdisj : Disjoint τ₁ τ₂) :
   279	    IsBall (insert t (τ₁ ∪ τ₂)) B := by
   280	  obtain ⟨l₁, hl₁, hn₁, hsh₁⟩ := h₁
   281	  obtain ⟨l₂, hl₂, hn₂, hsf₂⟩ := h₂
   282	  have htl₁ : t ∉ l₁ := fun hc => ht₁ (hl₁ ▸ List.mem_toFinset.mpr hc)
   283	  have htl₂ : t ∉ l₂ := fun hc => ht₂ (hl₂ ▸ List.mem_toFinset.mpr hc)
   284	  have hdl : l₁.Disjoint l₂ := by
   285	    intro a ha ha₂
   286	    exact Finset.disjoint_left.mp hdisj (hl₁ ▸ List.mem_toFinset.mpr ha)

codex
I’m going to answer as a review rather than patching: PM is a good fix for the triangle re-exposure defect, but the spec overclaims in a few places. The main hard stop is that H2’s existing type asks for transported shelling data, not just triangle incidence.

   142	/-- If the `W` capped side is the degree-3 star, then the opposite capped side has
   143	no apex vertex.  The two capped sides overlap only in the link triangle `γ`, and
   144	`v` is not a vertex of its own link. -/
   145	lemma degree3_apex_notMem_right_verts_of_left_star {σ : Finset (Finset V)}

   200	lemma degree3_hanchor (sigma sigmaR : Finset (Finset V)) (MR : Chain V)

   213	    (hsideVerts : sideVerts = vertsOf (insert γ (cutSet sigma W)))
   214	    (hMR_actual :

   216	    (hsigmaR_cut :
   217	      sigmaR = insert γ (cutSet sigma W) ∨

   219	    ∃ t₀ K,

codex
I would not rubber-stamp this. The plan is directionally right for the triangle re-exposure defect, but it overclaims in Q1/Q4.

**Bottom Line**

`IsPseudomanifold` is exactly the right invariant for “boundary triangle lies in one tet,” via `faceCount`/`IsPseudomanifold` in [Pseudomanifold.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Pseudomanifold.lean:22) and `faceCount_eq_one_of_boundary` in [Stickerball.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Stickerball.lean:58). But PM is not a substitute for all geometry in H2, and it is not the whole faithful stickerball invariant already present in the code: `IsStickerball` is defined as `FreelyShellable ∧ IsPseudomanifold ∧ EdgeLinkConnected` in [Pseudomanifold.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Pseudomanifold.lean:224).

So: PM probably suffices for H1’s missing “unique anchor tet” part. It does not, by itself, prove H2’s relative shelling bridge.

**Q1: Is EdgeLinkConnected Needed?**

For the two named sorries only: probably not for H1, unclear/unproven for H2.

For faithfulness of “stickerball” generally: yes, the current source explicitly says PM only controls triangle incidence, while edge links catch stray disconnected edge-link configurations. See the comment and definition around [Pseudomanifold.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Pseudomanifold.lean:76) and [Pseudomanifold.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Pseudomanifold.lean:99). If Peter’s objection is only “re-exposed triangle into a third tet,” PM handles that. If the formal target is “simplicial ball/stickerball” in the stronger local-manifold sense, dropping edge-link connectedness is not faithful to the file’s own design.

Concrete objection: `IsPseudomanifold τ := ∀ f, f.card = 3 → faceCount τ f ≤ 2` only counts triangles. It says nothing about connectedness of the link of an edge. The code already models that separately as `EdgeLinkConnected`.

**Q4a: H1 With PM**

H1 asks for much more than uniqueness of a tet containing `γ`; see [Theorem3.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Theorem3.lean:200). The target is:

```lean
∃ t₀ K,
  t₀ ∈ MR.support ∧
  GlueStep t₀ (tetFaces (starTet sigma v)) ((tetFaces t₀).erase γ ∪ K) ∧
  (∀ t ∈ MR.support, t ≠ t₀ → Disjoint (tetFaces t) (insert γ K)) ∧
  sigma = sigmaR.erase γ ∪ K
```

PM gives only this part:

```lean
faceCount MR.support γ = 1
```

provided you also have:
`γ.card = 3`, `γ ∈ sigmaR`, `UnitOn (bdry MR) sigmaR`, `SimplicialChain MR`, pure 4-tet support, and `IsPseudomanifold MR.support`.

That is enough to obtain unique `t₀ ∈ MR.support` with `γ ⊆ t₀`.

But the rest still needs real cut/star algebra:

1. `GlueStep t₀ (tetFaces star) ...`
   `GlueStep` requires shared face count 1 or 2 and exact symmetric-difference boundary, by definition in [Ball.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Ball.lean:63). PM does not prove the `newBdry` equality.

2. `rest_disj`
   Uniqueness of `γ` handles “no other tet touches `γ`.” Avoiding `K` uses `hvNotMR`, but only if every face in `K` contains `v`. That needs an explicit lemma tying `K` to the three star faces through `v`.

3. `sigma = sigmaR.erase γ ∪ K`
   This is pure cut geometry, not PM.

So H1 is likely provable with PM plus existing degree-3 cut facts, but the spec should not say “follows from faceCount = 1.” It follows from faceCount = 1 plus star/cut recovery lemmas. Also be careful with the proposed `K`; it should be the star boundary faces other than `γ`, i.e. the faces containing the apex `v`, not an ambiguous “exposed star faces” definition unless that is proved equal to `tetFaces (starTet σ v) \ {γ}`.

**Q4b: H2 With PM**

This is the bigger hole. H2 is not a PM lemma. Its target in [PrimeStep.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/PrimeStep.lean:1447) asks for:

```lean
GlueStep e σ₁ Bmid₁₂
GlueStep e σ₂ Bmid₂₁
RelShelling M₂.support Bmid₁₂ σ
RelShelling M₁.support Bmid₂₁ σ
```

`RelShelling` is an actual shelling list from an ambient boundary, not a counting condition; definition at [Ball.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Ball.lean:269). The transport lemma available, `ShellFrom_union_disjoint`, requires:

```lean
∀ t ∈ l, Disjoint (tetFaces t) K
```

see [Ball.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Ball.lean:166). PM does not produce that. At most PM says no triangle belongs to three tets. It does not identify the ambient carried piece `K`, does not prove `Bmid = σ₂ ∪ K`, and does not provide the disjointness needed to transport every glue step of the far side.

What you actually need for H2 is a “clean ambient transport” lemma:

```lean
Bmid₁₂ = σ₂ ∪ K₁₂
σ = final₂ ∪ K₁₂
∀ t ∈ M₂.support, Disjoint (tetFaces t) K₁₂
```

and symmetrically for the other side.

This may be provable from the side split facts already present, especially `A ∩ B = f₃ ∩ f₄` with card 2 and exact side separation from [PrimeStep.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/PrimeStep.lean:1163). If a far-side tet is contained in `B` and a carried triangle is contained in `A`, then a shared triangle would lie in `A ∩ B`, impossible because `(A ∩ B).card = 2`. But that is a side-cleanliness lemma, not a PM consequence.

So Q4b answer: PM is not enough. The missing fact is not edge-link connectedness either; it is explicit ambient-boundary cleanliness/disjointness for the side split, plus boundary algebra identifying the `Bmid`s. It may be a consequence of `hAB`, `hcd`, `hcover`, `hsep`, and the filtered boundary definitions, but it must be stated and proved.

**Q2: Is `Good` Avoidable?**

Probably yes for the PM invariant, but not using the current package as-is.

The side data already splits into bridge-independent pieces: `flipEdgePresent_side_sets` gives `A`, `B`, sphere sides, coverage, and separation at [PrimeStep.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/PrimeStep.lean:1163). `flipEdgePresent_side_algebra` then gives unit/closed/taut/simplicial/smaller/disjoint/support data. That is enough to apply IH to each side and union PM.

But `PrimeFlipEdgeSplitFreePackage` includes bridge fields `glue₁₂`, `glue₂₁`, `rel₂_over₁`, `rel₁_over₂` at [PrimeStep.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/PrimeStep.lean:48), and `flipEdgePresent_side_data` calls `flipEdgePresent_side_bridge` at [PrimeStep.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/PrimeStep.lean:1542). So Lemma B is circular if it consumes the current package.

**Q3: Factoring**

Yes, factor it. Add a bridge-free structure, something like:

```lean
PrimeFlipEdgeSplitCorePackage
```

containing fields through `side_coverage`, `side_separation`, `disjoint_supports`, `support_decomp`, `sphereᵢ`, `unitᵢ`, `tautᵢ`, `simpᵢ`, `smallerᵢ`.

Build it from `flipEdgePresent_side_sets` and `flipEdgePresent_side_algebra`. Then make the full `PrimeFlipEdgeSplitFreePackage` extend/use that plus `flipEdgePresent_side_bridge`.

**Q5: Architecture**

Use one modified induction returning a product, not a separate PM-only induction. The current `theorem3_core` IH only returns `FreelyShellable` ([Theorem23.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Theorem23.lean:100)), and both deg3/prime already need the same geometric setup. A separate PM induction would duplicate the hard branching and invite drift.

Recommended invariant if you only want to close the two triangle holes:

```lean
FreelyShellable M.support σ ∧ IsPseudomanifold M.support
```

Recommended invariant if the theorem is meant to be the file’s faithful stickerball:

```lean
IsStickerball M.support σ
```

But do not claim PM alone makes the formalization faithful unless you intentionally abandon the edge-link condition already encoded in `IsStickerball`.

Final adversarial verdict: PM is a necessary fix and probably closes H1’s real defect, but H2 still needs a bridge-cleanliness theorem. The spec is too optimistic when it says “PM available, holes discharged.”

**Q3: Factoring**

Yes, factor it. Add a bridge-free structure, something like:

```lean
PrimeFlipEdgeSplitCorePackage
```

containing fields through `side_coverage`, `side_separation`, `disjoint_supports`, `support_decomp`, `sphereᵢ`, `unitᵢ`, `tautᵢ`, `simpᵢ`, `smallerᵢ`.

Build it from `flipEdgePresent_side_sets` and `flipEdgePresent_side_algebra`. Then make the full `PrimeFlipEdgeSplitFreePackage` extend/use that plus `flipEdgePresent_side_bridge`.

**Q5: Architecture**

Use one modified induction returning a product, not a separate PM-only induction. The current `theorem3_core` IH only returns `FreelyShellable` ([Theorem23.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Theorem23.lean:100)), and both deg3/prime already need the same geometric setup. A separate PM induction would duplicate the hard branching and invite drift.

Recommended invariant if you only want to close the two triangle holes:

```lean
FreelyShellable M.support σ ∧ IsPseudomanifold M.support
```

Recommended invariant if the theorem is meant to be the file’s faithful stickerball:

```lean
IsStickerball M.support σ
```

But do not claim PM alone makes the formalization faithful unless you intentionally abandon the edge-link condition already encoded in `IsStickerball`.

Final adversarial verdict: PM is a necessary fix and probably closes H1’s real defect, but H2 still needs a bridge-cleanliness theorem. The spec is too optimistic when it says “PM available, holes discharged.”
